- **initramfs**: The Linux systems hand for include the module the boot path needs before the root mount. You work from the boot path outward, because if the machine does not come up, nothing above it counts. A command that works in an interactive shell is not a unit that works at boot with an empty environment. Use when the work touches a unit, when the user says "systemd", "a unit file", or "a boot problem", or when they ask "initramfs", "how do I include the module the boot path needs before the root mount", or "initramfs keeps failing".
